(optional)\"\n94\t              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n95\t              value={memo}\n96\t              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n97\t            />\n98\t\n99\t            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n100\t              <button\n101\t                className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n102\t                disabled={isPending}\n103\t                onClick={handleApprove}\n104\t              >\n105\t                Approve\n106\t              </button>\n107\t              <button\n108\t                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n109\t                disabled={isPending}\n110\t                onClick={handlePay}\n111\t              >\n112\t                Send Payout\n113\t              </button>\n114\t            </div>\n115\t          </>\n116\t        )}\n117\t\n118\t        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n119\t        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n120\t          {(payouts ?? `${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync, isPending } = useWriteContract();\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC.address,\n        abi: USDC.abi,\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [PAYOUTS.address, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6)],\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: PAYOUTS.address,\n        abi: PAYOUTS.abi,\n        functionName: \"pay\",\n        args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6), memo],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n            </p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                Approve\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n              {\" paid \"}\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>\n              {\" \"}\n              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","numLines":138,"startLine":1,"totalLines":138}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Bc8nkUUoPCZG9tHEu1WuDc","type":"tool_result","content":"# Template for NextJS environment variables.\n\n# For local development, copy this file, rename it to .env.local, and fill in the values.\n# When deploying live, you'll need to store the vars in Vercel/System config.\n\n# If not set, we provide default values (check `scaffold.config.ts`) so developers can start prototyping out of the box,\n# but we recommend getting your own API Keys for Production Apps.\n\n# To access the values stored in this env file you can use: process.env.VARIABLENAME\n# You'll need to prefix the variables names with NEXT_PUBLIC_ if you want to access them on the client side.\n# More info: https://nextjs.org/docs/pages/building-your-application/configuring/environment-variables\nNEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY=\nNEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID=\n\n===HH env===\n# Template for Hardhat environment variables.\n\n# To use this template, copy this file, rename it .env, and fill in the values.\n\n# If not set, we provide default values (check `hardhat.config.ts`) so developers can start prototyping out of the box,\n# but we recommend getting your own API Keys for Production Apps.\n\n# To access the values stored in this .env file you can use: process.env.VARIABLENAME\nALCHEMY_API_KEY=\nETHERSCAN_API_KEY=\n\n# Don't fill this value manually, run yarn generate to generate a new account or yarn account:import to import an existing PK.\nDEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY_ENCRYPTED=\n===utils formatUnits check: does page import correctly? check hooks===\nindex.ts\nuseContractLogs.ts\nuseCopyToClipboard.ts\nuseDeployedContractInfo.ts\nuseFetchBlocks.ts\nuseNetworkColor.ts\nuseOutsideClick.ts\nuseScaffoldContract.ts\nuseScaffoldEventHistory.ts\nuseScaffoldReadContract.ts","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b119473c-3802-46cd-bab8-aad1df4e3744","uuid":"d3f85311-64ca-4ee8-82a7-b7127cf76e72","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:30:10.148Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"# Template for NextJS environment variables.\n\n# For local development, copy this file, rename it to .env.local, and fill in the values.\n# When deploying live, you'll need to store the vars in Vercel/System config.\n\n# If not set, we provide default values (check `scaffold.config.ts`) so developers can start prototyping out of the box,\n# but we recommend getting your own API Keys for Production Apps.\n\n# To access the values stored in this env file you can use: process.env.VARIABLENAME\n# You'll need to prefix the variables names with NEXT_PUBLIC_ if you want to access them on the client side.\n# More info: https://nextjs.org/docs/pages/building-your-application/configuring/environment-variables\nNEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY=\nNEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID=\n\n===HH env===\n# Template for Hardhat environment variables.\n\n# To use this template, copy this file, rename it .env, and fill in the values.\n\n# If not set, we provide default values (check `hardhat.config.ts`) so developers can start prototyping out of the box,\n# but we recommend getting your own API Keys for Production Apps.\n\n# To access the values stored in this .env file you can use: process.env.VARIABLENAME\nALCHEMY_API_KEY=\nETHERSCAN_API_KEY=\n\n# Don't fill this value manually, run yarn generate to generate a new account or yarn account:import to import an existing PK.\nDEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY_ENCRYPTED=\n===utils formatUnits check: does page import correctly? check hooks===\nindex.ts\nuseContractLogs.ts\nuseCopyToClipboard.ts\nuseDeployedContractInfo.ts\nuseFetchBlocks.ts\nuseNetworkColor.ts\nuseOutsideClick.ts\nuseScaffoldContract.ts\nuseScaffoldEventHistory.ts\nuseScaffoldReadContract.ts","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

\"writeContractAsync\"\n> & {\n  isMining: boolean;\n  writeContractAsync: <\n    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n  >(\n    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n    options?: ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n  ) => Promise<WriteContractReturnType | undefined>;\n  writeContract: UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>,\n): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName>;\n/**\n * @deprecated Use object parameter version instead: useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"YourContract\" })\n */\nexport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n  contractName: TContractName,\n  writeContractParams?: UseWriteContractParameters,\n): in deployedContracts.ts & externalContracts.ts corresponding to targetNetworks configured in scaffold.config.ts\n * @param contractName - name of the contract to be written to\n * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n * @param writeContractParams - wagmi's useWriteContract parameters\n UseWriteContractParameters,\n): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName> {\n  const finalConfig =\n    typeof configOrName === \"string\"\n      ? { contractName: configOrName, writeContractParams, chainId: undefined }\n      : (configOrName as UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>);\n  const { contractName, chainId, writeContractParams: finalWriteContractParams } = finalConfig;\n\n  const wagmiConfig = useConfig();\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (typeof configOrName === \"string\") {\n      console.warn(\n        \"Using `useScaffoldWriteContract` with a string parameter is deprecated. Please use the object parameter version instead.\",\n      );\n    }\n  }, [configOrName]);\n\n  const { chain: accountChain } = useAccount();\n  const writeTx = useTransactor();\n  const [isMining, setIsMining] = useState(false);\n\n  const wagmiContractWrite = useWriteContract(finalWriteContractParams);\n\n  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n\n  const { data: deployedContractData } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n    contractName,\n    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n  });\n\n  const sendContractWriteAsyncTx = async <\n    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n  >(\n    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdM85TBnMBtK8AQXU1cWv","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now I understand the full picture. Here are the concrete issues to fix:\n\n1. **`fromBlock: 0n`** — scans ~33M Base blocks per poll; the payouts feed will hang/rate-limit. Use the contract's `deployedOnBlock`.\n2. **Raw `useWriteContract` + `console.error`** — users get zero feedback on success/failure of a *payment*. Switch to `useScaffoldWriteContract` (tx toasts, network checks, error notifications) per AGENTS.md.\n3. **No allowance awareness** — clicking \"Send Payout\" before approving just reverts. Make approval state-aware.\n4. **No recipient/amount validation** — bad input throws deep in wagmi.\n5. **`pollingInterval: 30000`** on an L2 — AGENTS.md says decrease for L2; feed lags.\n\nLet me rewrite the page."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1273,"cache_read_input_tokens":42296,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1273},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b119473c-3802-46cd-bab8-aad1df4e3744","uuid":"1dde98e5-fb2a-4aaa-8a5d-a2ecacd0c37c","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:31:18.898Z","request_id":"req_011CdM85S6JBYbXHsdgL3DGj"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdM85TBnMBtK8AQXU1cWv","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01AyMTrpZZoeeAKoHpYxM6Wg","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T152855Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useMemo, useState } from \"react\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, isAddress, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PAYOUTS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts;\n\nconst shorten = (addr?: string) => (addr ? `${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writePayouts, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n  });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS.address],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: BigInt(PAYOUTS.deployedOnBlock),\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  // Parse the amount to USDC's 6 decimals. Returns undefined for empty/invalid input.\n  const parsedAmount = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!amount) return undefined;\n    try {\n      return parseUnits(amount, 6);\n    } catch {\n      return undefined;\n    }\n  }, [amount]);\n\n  const isValidRecipient = isAddress(recipient);\n  const needsApproval = parsedAmount !== undefined && (allowance ?? 0n) < parsedAmount;\n  const hasEnoughBalance = parsedAmount !== undefined && (usdcBalance ?? 0n) >= parsedAmount;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (parsedAmount === undefined || parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount to approve.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    try {\n      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [PAYOUTS.address, parsedAmount] });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (!isValidRecipient) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid recipient address.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (parsedAmount === undefined || parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    try {\n      await writePayouts({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC</p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-1\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            {recipient && !isValidRecipient && <p className=\"text-error text-sm mb-2\">Invalid address.</p>}\n\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mt-2 mb-1\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            {parsedAmount !== undefined && !hasEnoughBalance && (\n              <p className=\"text-error text-sm mb-2\">Insufficient USDC balance.</p>\n            )}\n\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mt-2 mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isApproving ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isApproving || parsedAmount === undefined || !needsApproval}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                {needsApproval ? \"Approve\" : \"Approved\"}\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPaying ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPaying || !isValidRecipient || parsedAmount === undefined || needsApproval || !hasEnoughBalance}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n              {\" paid \"}\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>{\" \"}\n              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1273,"cache_read_input_tokens":42296,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1273},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b119473c-3802-46cd-bab8-aad1df4e3744","uuid":"2be29661-bd1b-4018-9024-3ddda2d4d4d1","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:31:40.957Z","request_id":"req_011CdM85S6JBYbXHsdgL3DGj"}

`${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync, isPending } = useWriteContract();\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC.address,\n        abi: USDC.abi,\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [PAYOUTS.address, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6)],\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: PAYOUTS.address,\n        abi: PAYOUTS.abi,\n        functionName: \"pay\",\n        args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6), memo],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n            </p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                Approve\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n              {\" paid \"}\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>\n              {\" \"}\n              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","userModified":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ShUqA9GoTmpCXowP4aNygV","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"use client\";\n2\t\n3\timport { useMemo, useState } from \"react\";\n4\timport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\n5\timport { formatUnits, isAddress, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\n6\timport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\n7\timport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\n8\timport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n9\timport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n10\t\n11\tconst PAYOUTS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts;\n12\t\n13\tconst shorten = (addr?: string) => (addr ? `${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n14\t\n15\tconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n16\t  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n17\t  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n18\t  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n19\t  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n20\t\n21\t  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n22\t  const { writeContractAsync: writePayouts, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n23\t    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n24\t  });\n25\t\n26\t  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n27\t    contractName: \"USDC\",\n28\t    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n29\t    args: [connectedAddress],\n30\t  });\n31\t\n32\t  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n33\t    contractName: \"USDC\",\n34\t    functionName: \"allowance\",\n35\t    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS.address],\n36\t  });\n37\t\n38\t  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n39\t    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n40\t    eventName: \"Paid\",\n41\t    fromBlock: BigInt(PAYOUTS.deployedOnBlock),\n42\t    watch: true,\n43\t  });\n44\t\n45\t  // Parse the amount to USDC's 6 decimals. Returns undefined for empty/invalid input.\n46\t  const parsedAmount = useMemo(() => {\n47\t    if (!amount) return undefined;\n48\t    try {\n49\t      return parseUnits(amount, 6);\n50\t    } catch {\n51\t      return undefined;\n52\t    }\n53\t  }, [amount]);\n54\t\n55\t  const isValidRecipient = isAddress(recipient);\n56\t  const needsApproval = parsedAmount !== undefined && (allowance ?? 0n) < parsedAmount;\n57\t  const hasEnoughBalance = parsedAmount !== undefined && (usdcBalance ?? 0n) >= parsedAmount;\n58\t\n59\t  const handleApprove = async () => {\n60\t    if (parsedAmount === undefined || parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n61\t      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount to approve.\");\n62\t      return;\n63\t    }\n64\t    try {\n65\t      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [PAYOUTS.address, parsedAmount] });\n66\t    } catch (e) {\n67\t      console.error(e);\n68\t    }\n69\t  };\n70\t\n71\t  const handlePay = async () => {\n72\t    if (!isValidRecipient) {\n73\t      notification.error(\"Enter a valid recipient address.\");\n74\t      return;\n75\t    }\n76\t    if (parsedAmount === undefined || parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n77\t      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount.\");\n78\t      return;\n79\t    }\n80\t    try {\n81\t      await writePayouts({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n82\t      setAmount(\"\");\n83\t      setMemo(\"\");\n84\t    } catch (e) {\n85\t      console.error(e);\n86\t    }\n87\t  };\n88\t\n89\t  return (\n90\t    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n91\t      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n92\t        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n93\t        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n94\t\n95\t        {!isConnected ? (\n96\t          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n97\t        ) : (\n98\t          <>\n99\t            <p className=\"mb-4\">Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC</p>\n100\t\n101\t            <input\n102\t              type=\"text\"\n103\t              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n104\t              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-1\"\n105\t              value={recipient}\n106\t              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n107\t            />\n108\t            {recipient && !isValidRecipient && <p className=\"text-error text-sm mb-2\">Invalid address.</p>}\n109\t\n110\t            <input\n111\t              type=\"number\"\n112\t              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n113\t              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mt-2 mb-1\"\n114\t              value={amount}\n115\t              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n116\t            />\n117\t            {parsedAmount !== undefined && !hasEnoughBalance && (\n118\t              <p className=\"text-error text-sm mb-2\">Insufficient USDC balance.</p>\n119\t            )}\n120\t\n121\t            <textarea\n122\t              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n123\t              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mt-2 mb-4\"\n124\t              value={memo}\n125\t              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n126\t            />\n127\t\n128\t            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n129\t              <button\n130\t                className={`btn btn-primary ${isApproving ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n131\t                disabled={isApproving || parsedAmount === undefined || !needsApproval}\n132\t                onClick={handleApprove}\n133\t              >\n134\t                {needsApproval ? \"Approve\" : \"Approved\"}\n135\t              </button>\n136\t              <button\n137\t                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPaying ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n138\t                disabled={\n139\t                  isPaying || !isValidRecipient || parsedAmount === undefined || needsApproval || !hasEnoughBalance\n140\t                }\n141\t                onClick={handlePay}\n142\t              >\n143\t                Send Payout\n144\t              </button>\n145\t            </div>\n146\t          </>\n147\t        )}\n148\t\n149\t        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n150\t        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n151\t          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n152\t            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n153\t              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n154\t              {\" paid \"}\n155\t              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>{\" \"}\n156\t              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n157\t              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n158\t            </div>\n159\t          ))}\n160\t          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n161\t        </div>\n162\t      </div>\n163\t    </div>\n164\t  );\n165\t};\n166\t\n167\texport default PayoutsPage;\n168\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01YSkLzTW2CPXNTPrasUmUsS","session_id":"b119473c-3802-46cd-bab8-aad1df4e3744","uuid":"ac9c12da-4c0b-4a95-8b41-7afa4b7b7a5d","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:33:26.187Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review payouts changes"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AuxDYizj97Azp9JA1vhXJk","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport * as chains from \"viem/chains\";\n2\t\n3\t\n4\t\n5\texport type BaseConfig = {\n6\t  targetNetworks: readonly chains.Chain[];\n7\t  pollingInterval: number;\n8\t  alchemyApiKey: string;\n9\t  rpcOverrides?: Record<number, string>;\n10\t  walletConnectProjectId: string;\n11\t  burnerWalletMode: \"localNetworksOnly\" | \"allNetworks\" | \"disabled\";\n12\t};\n13\t\n14\texport type ScaffoldConfig = BaseConfig ;\n15\t\n16\texport const DEFAULT_ALCHEMY_API_KEY = \"IZYEU2cWBgnFmgiTAgpWD\";\n17\t\n18\tconst scaffoldConfig = {\n19\t  // The networks on which your DApp is live\n20\t  targetNetworks: [\n21\t    chains.base\n22\t  ],\n23\t  // The interval at which your front-end polls the RPC servers for new data (it has no effect if you only target the local network (default is 4000))\n24\t  // Base is an L2 with ~2s blocks, so keep polling snappy for a near-real-time payouts feed.\n25\t  pollingInterval: 4000,\n26\t  // This is ours Alchemy's default API key.\n27\t  // You can get your own at https://dashboard.alchemyapi.io\n28\t  // It's recommended to store it in an env variable:\n29\t  // .env.local for local testing, and in the Vercel/system env config for live apps.\n30\t  alchemyApiKey: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY || DEFAULT_ALCHEMY_API_KEY,\n31\t  // If you want to use a different RPC for a specific network, you can add it here.\n32\t  // The key is the chain ID, and the value is the HTTP RPC URL\n33\t  rpcOverrides: {\n34\t    // Example:\n35\t    // [chains.mainnet.id]: \"https://mainnet.rpc.buidlguidl.com\",\n36\t  },\n37\t  // This is ours WalletConnect's default project ID.\n38\t  // You can get your own at https://cloud.walletconnect.com\n39\t  // It's recommended to store it in an env variable:\n40\t  // .env.local for local testing, and in the Vercel/system env config for live apps.\n41\t  walletConnectProjectId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID || '3a8170812b534d0ff9d794f19a901d64',\n42\t  // Configure Burner Wallet visibility:\n43\t  // - \"localNetworksOnly\": only show when all target networks are local (hardhat/anvil)\n44\t  // - \"allNetworks\": show on any configured target networks\n45\t  // - \"disabled\": completely disable\n46\t  burnerWalletMode: 'localNetworksOnly'\n47\t} as const satisfies ScaffoldConfig;\n48\t\n49\texport default scaffoldConfig;"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01YSkLzTW2CPXNTPrasUmUsS","session_id":"b119473c-3802-46cd-bab8-aad1df4e3744","uuid":"fbd7d99a-5d51-4a8e-aa49-12dfb25db6f7","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:33:36.092Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AnmQ5qk5PF82V1mCThA8P7","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n2\tpragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n3\t\n4\tinterface IERC20 {\n5\t    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool);\n6\t}\n7\t\n8\t/**\n9\t * Routes USDC payouts straight to recipients and records them for the feed.\n10\t */\n11\tcontract Payouts {\n12\t    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n13\t\n14\t    event Paid(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 amount, string memo);\n15\t\n16\t    constructor(address _usdc) {\n17\t        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n18\t    }\n19\t\n20\t    function pay(address to, uint256 amount, string calldata memo) external {\n21\t        require(to != address(0), \"Payouts: zero recipient\");\n22\t        require(amount > 0, \"Payouts: zero amount\");\n23\t        require(usdc.transferFrom(msg.sender, to, amount), \"Payouts: transfer failed\");\n24\t        emit Paid(msg.sender, to, amount, memo);\n25\t    }\n26\t}\n27\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01YSkLzTW2CPXNTPrasUmUsS","session_id":"b119473c-3802-46cd-bab8-aad1df4e3744","uuid":"ee56f772-d2da-439b-841f-8bd2e43f426f","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:33:36.191Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review payouts changes"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01L2pJVMpiy32FK2FGBxsi4z","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\n2\timport { MutateOptions } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\n3\timport { Abi, ExtractAbiFunctionNames } from \"abitype\";\n4\timport { Config, UseWriteContractParameters, useAccount, useConfig, useWriteContract } from \"wagmi\";\n5\timport { WriteContractErrorType, WriteContractReturnType } from \"wagmi/actions\";\n6\timport { WriteContractVariables } from \"wagmi/query\";\n7\timport { useSelectedNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n8\timport { useDeployedContractInfo, useTransactor } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n9\timport { AllowedChainIds, notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n10\timport {\n11\t  ContractAbi,\n12\t  ContractName,\n13\t  ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n14\t  ScaffoldWriteContractVariables,\n15\t  UseScaffoldWriteConfig,\n16\t  simulateContractWriteAndNotifyError,\n17\t} from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n18\t\n19\ttype ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName extends ContractName> = Omit<\n20\t  ReturnType<typeof useWriteContract>,\n21\t  \"writeContract\" | \"writeContractAsync\"\n22\t> & {\n23\t  isMining: boolean;\n24\t  writeContractAsync: <\n25\t    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n26\t  >(\n27\t    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n28\t    options?: ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n29\t  ) => Promise<WriteContractReturnType | undefined>;\n30\t  writeContract: <TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">>(\n31\t    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n32\t    options?: Omit<ScaffoldWriteContractOptions, \"onBlockConfirmation\" | \"blockConfirmations\">,\n33\t  ) => void;\n34\t};\n35\t\n36\texport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n37\t  config: UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>,\n38\t): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName>;\n39\t/**\n40\t * @deprecated Use object parameter version instead: useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"YourContract\" })\n41\t */\n42\texport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n43\t  contractName: TContractName,\n44\t  writeContractParams?: UseWriteContractParameters,\n45\t): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName>;\n46\t\n47\t/**\n48\t * Wrapper around wagmi's useWriteContract hook which automatically loads (by name) the contract ABI and address from\n49\t * the contracts present in deployedContracts.ts & externalContracts.ts corresponding to targetNetworks configured in scaffold.config.ts\n50\t * @param contractName - name of the contract to be written to\n51\t * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n52\t * @param writeContractParams - wagmi's useWriteContract parameters\n53\t */\n54\texport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n55\t  configOrName: UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName> | TContractName,\n56\t  writeContractParams?: UseWriteContractParameters,\n57\t): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName> {\n58\t  const finalConfig =\n59\t    typeof configOrName === \"string\"\n60\t      ? { contractName: configOrName, writeContractParams, chainId: undefined }\n61\t      : (configOrName as UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>);\n62\t  const { contractName, chainId, writeContractParams: finalWriteContractParams } = finalConfig;\n63\t\n64\t  const wagmiConfig = useConfig();\n65\t\n66\t  useEffect(() => {\n67\t    if (typeof configOrName === \"string\") {\n68\t      console.warn(\n69\t        \"Using `useScaffoldWriteContract` with a string parameter is deprecated. Please use the object parameter version instead.\",\n70\t      );\n71\t    }\n72\t  }, [configOrName]);\n73\t\n74\t  const { chain: accountChain } = useAccount();\n75\t  const writeTx = useTransactor();\n76\t  const [isMining, setIsMining] = useState(false);\n77\t\n78\t  const wagmiContractWrite = useWriteContract(finalWriteContractParams);\n79\t\n80\t  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n81\t\n82\t  const { data: deployedContractData } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n83\t    contractName,\n84\t    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n85\t  });\n86\t\n87\t  const sendContractWriteAsyncTx = async <\n88\t    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n89\t  >(\n90\t    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n91\t    options?: ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n92\t  ) => {\n93\t    if (!deployedContractData) {\n94\t      notification.error(\"Target Contract is not deployed, did you forget to run `yarn deploy`?\");\n95\t      return;\n96\t    }\n97\t\n98\t    if (!accountChain?.id) {\n99\t      notification.error(\"Please connect your wallet\");\n100\t      return;\n101\t    }\n102\t\n103\t    if (accountChain?.id !== selectedNetwork.id) {\n104\t      notification.error(`Wallet is connected to the wrong network. Please switch to ${selectedNetwork.name}`);\n105\t      return;\n106\t    }\n107\t\n108\t    try {\n109\t      setIsMining(true);\n110\t      const { blockConfirmations, onBlockConfirmation, ...mutateOptions } = options || {};\n111\t\n112\t      const writeContractObject = {\n113\t        abi: deployedContractData.abi as Abi,\n114\t        address: deployedContractData.address,\n115\t        ...variables,\n116\t      } as WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>;\n117\t\n118\t      if (!finalConfig?.disableSimulate) {\n119\t        await simulateContractWriteAndNotifyError({\n120\t          wagmiConfig,\n121\t          writeContractParams: writeContractObject,\n122\t          chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n123\t        });\n124\t      }\n125\t\n126\t      const makeWriteWithParams = () =>\n127\t        wagmiContractWrite.writeContractAsync(\n128\t          writeContractObject,\n129\t          mutateOptions as\n130\t            | MutateOptions<\n131\t                WriteContractReturnType,\n132\t                WriteContractErrorType,\n133\t                WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>,\n134\t                unknown\n135\t              >\n136\t            | undefined,\n137\t        );\n138\t      const writeTxResult = await writeTx(makeWriteWithParams, { blockConfirmations, onBlockConfirmation });\n139\t\n140\t      return writeTxResult;\n141\t    } catch (e: any) {\n142\t      throw e;\n143\t    } finally {\n144\t      setIsMining(false);\n145\t    }\n146\t  };\n147\t\n148\t  const sendContractWriteTx = <\n149\t    TContractName extends ContractName,\n150\t    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n151\t  >(\n152\t    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n153\t    options?: Omit<ScaffoldWriteContractOptions, \"onBlockConfirmation\" | \"blockConfirmations\">,\n154\t  ) => {\n155\t    if (!deployedContractData) {\n156\t      notification.error(\"Target Contract is not deployed, did you forget to run `yarn deploy`?\");\n157\t      return;\n158\t    }\n159\t    if (!accountChain?.id) {\n160\t      notification.error(\"Please connect your wallet\");\n161\t      return;\n162\t    }\n163\t\n164\t    if (accountChain?.id !== selectedNetwork.id) {\n165\t      notification.error(`Wallet is connected to the wrong network. Please switch to ${selectedNetwork.name}`);\n166\t      return;\n167\t    }\n168\t\n169\t    wagmiContractWrite.writeContract(\n170\t      {\n171\t        abi: deployedContractData.abi as Abi,\n172\t        address: deployedContractData.address,\n173\t        ...variables,\n174\t      } as WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>,\n175\t      options as\n176\t        | MutateOptions<\n177\t            WriteContractReturnType,\n178\t            WriteContractErrorType,\n179\t            WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>,\n180\t            unknown\n181\t          >\n182\t        | undefined,\n183\t    );\n184\t  };\n185\t\n186\t  return {\n187\t    ...wagmiContractWrite,\n188\t    isMining,\n189\t    // Overwrite wagmi's writeContactAsync\n190\t    writeContractAsync: sendContractWriteAsyncTx,\n191\t    // Overwrite wagmi's writeContract\n192\t    writeContract: sendContractWriteTx,\n193\t  };\n194\t}\n195\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01YSkLzTW2CPXNTPrasUmUsS","session_id":"b119473c-3802-46cd-bab8-aad1df4e3744","uuid":"6a5b4ade-8c62-485a-8a7b-3ae78f21bacc","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:34:13.580Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review payouts changes"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0169wDJfwriqS9gqJjoNvbcK","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\n2\timport { useInfiniteQuery, useQuery } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\n3\timport { Abi, AbiEvent, ExtractAbiEventNames } from \"abitype\";\n4\timport { BlockNumber, GetLogsParameters } from \"viem\";\n5\timport { hardhat } from \"viem/chains\";\n6\timport { Config, UsePublicClientReturnType, useBlockNumber, usePublicClient } from \"wagmi\";\n7\timport { useSelectedNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n8\timport { useDeployedContractInfo } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n9\timport { AllowedChainIds } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n10\timport { replacer } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/common\";\n11\timport {\n12\t  ContractAbi,\n13\t  ContractName,\n14\t  UseScaffoldEventHistoryConfig,\n15\t  UseScaffoldEventHistoryData,\n16\t} from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n17\t\n18\tconst getEvents = async (\n19\t  getLogsParams: GetLogsParameters<AbiEvent | undefined, AbiEvent[] | undefined, boolean, BlockNumber, BlockNumber>,\n20\t  publicClient?: UsePublicClientReturnType<Config, number>,\n21\t  Options?: {\n22\t    blockData?: boolean;\n23\t    transactionData?: boolean;\n24\t    receiptData?: boolean;\n25\t  },\n26\t) => {\n27\t  const logs = await publicClient?.getLogs({\n28\t    address: getLogsParams.address,\n29\t    fromBlock: getLogsParams.fromBlock,\n30\t    toBlock: getLogsParams.toBlock,\n31\t    args: getLogsParams.args,\n32\t    event: getLogsParams.event,\n33\t  });\n34\t  if (!logs) return undefined;\n35\t\n36\t  const finalEvents = await Promise.all(\n37\t    logs.map(async log => {\n38\t      return {\n39\t        ...log,\n40\t        blockData:\n41\t          Options?.blockData && log.blockHash ? await publicClient?.getBlock({ blockHash: log.blockHash }) : null,\n42\t        transactionData:\n43\t          Options?.transactionData && log.transactionHash\n44\t            ? await publicClient?.getTransaction({ hash: log.transactionHash })\n45\t            : null,\n46\t        receiptData:\n47\t          Options?.receiptData && log.transactionHash\n48\t            ? await publicClient?.getTransactionReceipt({ hash: log.transactionHash })\n49\t            : null,\n50\t      };\n51\t    }),\n52\t  );\n53\t\n54\t  return finalEvents;\n55\t};\n56\t\n57\t/**\n58\t * @deprecated **Recommended only for local (hardhat/anvil) chains and development.**\n59\t * It uses getLogs which can overload RPC endpoints (especially on L2s with short block times).\n60\t * For production, use an indexer such as ponder.sh or similar to query contract events efficiently."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01YSkLzTW2CPXNTPrasUmUsS","session_id":"b119473c-3802-46cd-bab8-aad1df4e3744","uuid":"dff28f03-b6c1-4e71-a4e9-a30ca5deebe7","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:34:14.738Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review payouts changes"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TCRdL7tuc2AhoJeJs3dWTD","type":"tool_result","content":"60\t * For production, use an indexer such as ponder.sh or similar to query contract events efficiently.\n61\t *\n62\t * Reads events from a deployed contract.\n63\t * @param config - The config settings\n64\t * @param config.contractName - deployed contract name\n65\t * @param config.eventName - name of the event to listen for\n66\t * @param config.fromBlock - optional block number to start reading events from (defaults to `deployedOnBlock` in deployedContracts.ts if set for contract, otherwise defaults to 0)\n67\t * @param config.toBlock - optional block number to stop reading events at (if not provided, reads until current block)\n68\t * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n69\t * @param config.filters - filters to be applied to the event (parameterName: value)\n70\t * @param config.blockData - if set to true it will return the block data for each event (default: false)\n71\t * @param config.transactionData - if set to true it will return the transaction data for each event (default: false)\n72\t * @param config.receiptData - if set to true it will return the receipt data for each event (default: false)\n73\t * @param config.watch - if set to true, the events will be updated every pollingInterval milliseconds set at scaffoldConfig (default: false)\n74\t * @param config.enabled - set this to false to disable the hook from running (default: true)\n75\t * @param config.blocksBatchSize - optional batch size for fetching events. If specified, each batch will contain at most this many blocks (default: 500)\n76\t */\n77\texport const useScaffoldEventHistory = <\n78\t  TContractName extends ContractName,\n79\t  TEventName extends ExtractAbiEventNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>>,\n80\t  TBlockData extends boolean = false,\n81\t  TTransactionData extends boolean = false,\n82\t  TReceiptData extends boolean = false,\n83\t>({\n84\t  contractName,\n85\t  eventName,\n86\t  fromBlock,\n87\t  toBlock,\n88\t  chainId,\n89\t  filters,\n90\t  blockData,\n91\t  transactionData,\n92\t  receiptData,\n93\t  watch,\n94\t  enabled = true,\n95\t  blocksBatchSize = 500,\n96\t}: UseScaffoldEventHistoryConfig<TContractName, TEventName, TBlockData, TTransactionData, TReceiptData>) => {\n97\t  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n98\t\n99\t  // Runtime warning for non-local chains\n100\t  useEffect(() => {\n101\t    if (selectedNetwork.id !== hardhat.id) {\n102\t      console.log(\n103\t        \"⚠️ useScaffoldEventHistory is not optimized for production use. It can overload RPC endpoints (especially on L2s)\",\n104\t      );\n105\t    }\n106\t  }, [selectedNetwork.id]);\n107\t\n108\t  const publicClient = usePublicClient({\n109\t    chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n110\t  });\n111\t  const [liveEvents, setLiveEvents] = useState<any[]>([]);\n112\t  const [lastFetchedBlock, setLastFetchedBlock] = useState<bigint | null>(null);\n113\t  const [isPollingActive, setIsPollingActive] = useState(false);\n114\t\n115\t  const { data: blockNumber } = useBlockNumber({ watch: watch, chainId: selectedNetwork.id });\n116\t\n117\t  const { data: deployedContractData } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n118\t    contractName,\n119\t    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n120\t  });\n121\t\n122\t  const event =\n123\t    deployedContractData &&\n124\t    ((deployedContractData.abi as Abi).find(part => part.type === \"event\" && part.name === eventName) as AbiEvent);\n125\t\n126\t  const isContractAddressAndClientReady = Boolean(deployedContractData?.address) && Boolean(publicClient);\n127\t\n128\t  const fromBlockValue =\n129\t    fromBlock !== undefined\n130\t      ? fromBlock\n131\t      : BigInt(\n132\t          deployedContractData && \"deployedOnBlock\" in deployedContractData\n133\t            ? deployedContractData.deployedOnBlock || 0\n134\t            : 0,\n135\t        );\n136\t\n137\t  const query = useInfiniteQuery({\n138\t    queryKey: [\n139\t      \"eventHistory\",\n140\t      {\n141\t        contractName,\n142\t        address: deployedContractData?.address,\n143\t        eventName,\n144\t        fromBlock: fromBlockValue?.toString(),\n145\t        toBlock: toBlock?.toString(),\n146\t        chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n147\t        filters: JSON.stringify(filters, replacer),\n148\t        blocksBatchSize: blocksBatchSize.toString(),\n149\t      },\n150\t    ],\n151\t    queryFn: async ({ pageParam }) => {\n152\t      if (!isContractAddressAndClientReady) return undefined;\n153\t\n154\t      // Calculate the toBlock for this batch\n155\t      let batchToBlock = toBlock;\n156\t      const batchEndBlock = pageParam + BigInt(blocksBatchSize) - 1n;\n157\t      const maxBlock = toBlock || (blockNumber ? BigInt(blockNumber) : undefined);\n158\t      if (maxBlock) {\n159\t        batchToBlock = batchEndBlock < maxBlock ? batchEndBlock : maxBlock;\n160\t      }\n161\t\n162\t      const data = await getEvents(\n163\t        {\n164\t          address: deployedContractData?.address,\n165\t          event,\n166\t          fromBlock: pageParam,\n167\t          toBlock: batchToBlock,\n168\t          args: filters,\n169\t        },\n170\t        publicClient,\n171\t        { blockData, transactionData, receiptData },\n172\t      );\n173\t\n174\t      setLastFetchedBlock(batchToBlock || blockNumber || 0n);\n175\t\n176\t      return data;\n177\t    },\n178\t    enabled: enabled && isContractAddressAndClientReady && !isPollingActive, // Disable when polling starts\n179\t    initialPageParam: fromBlockValue,\n180\t    getNextPageParam: (lastPage, allPages, lastPageParam) => {\n181\t      if (!blockNumber || fromBlockValue >= blockNumber) return undefined;\n182\t\n183\t      const nextBlock = lastPageParam + BigInt(blocksBatchSize);\n184\t\n185\t      // Don't go beyond the specified toBlock or current block\n186\t      const maxBlock = toBlock && toBlock < blockNumber ? toBlock : blockNumber;\n187\t\n188\t      if (nextBlock > maxBlock) return undefined;\n189\t\n190\t      return nextBlock;\n191\t    },\n192\t    select: data => {\n193\t      const events = data.pages.flat() as unknown as UseScaffoldEventHistoryData<\n194\t        TContractName,\n195\t        TEventName,\n196\t        TBlockData,\n197\t        TTransactionData,\n198\t        TReceiptData\n199\t      >;\n200\t\n201\t      return {\n202\t        pages: events?.reverse(),\n203\t        pageParams: data.pageParams,\n204\t      };\n205\t    },\n206\t  });\n207\t\n208\t  // Check if we're caught up and should start polling\n209\t  const shouldStartPolling = () => {\n210\t    if (!watch || !blockNumber || isPollingActive) return false;\n211\t\n212\t    return !query.hasNextPage && query.status === \"success\";\n213\t  };\n214\t\n215\t  // Poll for new events when watch mode is enabled\n216\t  useQuery({\n217\t    queryKey: [\"liveEvents\", contractName, eventName, blockNumber?.toString(), lastFetchedBlock?.toString()],\n218\t    enabled: Boolean(\n219\t      watch && enabled && isContractAddressAndClientReady && blockNumber && (shouldStartPolling() || isPollingActive),\n220\t    ),\n221\t    queryFn: async () => {\n222\t      if (!isContractAddressAndClientReady || !blockNumber) return null;\n223\t\n224\t      if (!isPollingActive && shouldStartPolling()) {\n225\t        setIsPollingActive(true);\n226\t      }\n227\t\n228\t      const maxBlock = toBlock && toBlock < blockNumber ? toBlock : blockNumber;\n229\t      const startBlock = lastFetchedBlock || maxBlock;\n230\t\n231\t      // Only fetch if there are new blocks to check\n232\t      if (startBlock >= maxBlock) return null;\n233\t\n234\t      const newEvents = await getEvents(\n235\t        {\n236\t          address: deployedContractData?.address,\n237\t          event,\n238\t          fromBlock: startBlock + 1n,\n239\t          toBlock: maxBlock,"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01YSkLzTW2CPXNTPrasUmUsS","session_id":"b119473c-3802-46cd-bab8-aad1df4e3744","uuid":"9d275837-d339-4272-8810-74d2a60e08dd","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:34:37.462Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review payouts changes"}

{"type":"system","subtype":"task_notification","task_id":"a19c5cea8ed4bd2ef","tool_use_id":"toolu_01YSkLzTW2CPXNTPrasUmUsS","status":"completed","output_file":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-liana-Documents-prog-buidl-guidl-ethskills-evals-artifacts-qa-goal-002-2026-07-24T152855Z-claude-no-skill-2-workspace/b119473c-3802-46cd-bab8-aad1df4e3744/tasks/a19c5cea8ed4bd2ef.output","summary":"Reviewed all three files plus the deployedContracts entries and the two hooks the page relies on. The rewrite is a genuine improvement and the core logic is correct — approve-to-`PAYOUTS.address` as spender, `transferFrom` pull pattern, 6-decimal parsing, allowance/balance gating all line up with the contract and the real Base USDC (`0x8335…2913`, 6 decimals). No funds-loss or critical logic bug.\n\nFindings, most-to-least important:\n\n**Ship-readiness (not a code bug, but the real risk for a mainnet launch this week)**\n- `useScaffoldEventHistory` on Base mainnet with `watch: true` is the documented anti-pattern. The hook itself logs \"not optimized for production… can overload RPC endpoints (especially on L2s)\" (`useScaffoldEventHistory.ts:58-60,100-106`) and recommends an indexer. Your `fromBlock` fix helps the backfill, but on load it still does sequential 500-block `getLogs` batches from block 33712450 to tip, then polls every 4s. On the shared default Alchemy key this is a rate-limit / broken-feed risk under real traffic. Consider ponder/subgraph for the feed, or at minimum your own dedicated RPC key. This is the thing I'd resolve before shipping.\n- `scaffold.config.ts:25` — `pollingInterval: 4000` is **global**, not scoped to the payouts feed. It quadruples-plus the RPC call rate for every read/block-watch in the whole app. Reasonable for a live feed, but combine with the point above and the shared key and you may hit limits. Fine if you ship your own key.\n- `scaffold.config.ts:16,30,41` — still using `DEFAULT_ALCHEMY_API_KEY` and the default WalletConnect projectId as fallbacks. For real users, set `NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY` and `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID` in Vercel before launch. Verify this is done — the defaults are shared/throttled.\n- `deployedContracts.ts:48` — `deployedOnBlock: 33712450` is load-bearing for the feed's backfill. Confirm it's the actual deploy block; if it's set too high, historical payouts silently won't show.\n\n**Minor (not blockers)**\n- `page.tsx:57,117` — while `usdcBalance` is still loading (`undefined`), `hasEnoughBalance` computes `0n >= parsedAmount` → false, so a valid user briefly sees \"Insufficient USDC balance\" and a disabled Send button until the read resolves. Gate the message on `usdcBalance !== undefined` to avoid the false-negative flash.\n- `page.tsx:134` — Approve button shows \"Approved\" when no amount is entered (`needsApproval` is false for empty input). Cosmetic; button is disabled anyway.\n- `page.tsx:67,84` — `catch { console.error }` is redundant: `useScaffoldWriteContract` already surfaces errors via notifications/simulation. Harmless, just dead-ish code.\n- `page.tsx:152` — `key={i}` on a reversed, prepend-on-watch list causes React to re-key existing rows as new events arrive. Prefer a stable key like `${payout.transactionHash}-${payout.logIndex}`.\n\nNet: functionally correct and a clear upgrade over the previous version. No code-level ship blocker, but I would not call it launch-ready until the mainnet event-indexing/RPC-key story (points 1–3) is addressed, since that's what will actually break for real users under load.\n\nRelevant files:\n- `/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T152855Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx`\n- `/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T152855Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`\n- `/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T152855Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts`","usage":{"total_tokens":27297,"tool_uses":8,"duration_ms":174569},"uuid":"ba1960d1-7d45-4598-8529-972d7349dafe","session_id":"b119473c-3802-46cd-bab8-aad1df4e3744"}

Focus on correctness/ship-readiness, not nitpicks.\n\nFiles changed:\n1. packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx — full rewrite\n2. packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts — pollingInterval 30000 -> 4000\n\nContext of the rewrite: previously the page used raw wagmi `useWriteContract` with only `console.error` (no user feedback), scanned events `fromBlock: 0n` (~33M Base blocks per poll), and had no allowance/address/balance validation. I switched to `useScaffoldWriteContract` (tx toasts + notifications + network checks), `fromBlock: BigInt(PAYOUTS.deployedOnBlock)`, added allowance-aware Approve button, recipient `isAddress` validation, balance check, and safe amount parsing.\n\nThe Payouts contract (packages/hardhat/contracts/Payouts.sol) is already deployed and its `pay(address,uint256,string)` does `usdc.transferFrom(msg.sender, to, amount)`.\n\nRead the two changed files and Payouts.sol. 0n) < parsedAmount;\n  const hasEnoughBalance = parsedAmount !== undefined && (usdcBalance ?? 0n) >= parsedAmount;","newString":"  const isValidRecipient = isAddress(recipient);\n  const needsApproval = parsedAmount !== undefined && (allowance ?? 0n) < parsedAmount;\n  // Undefined balance means the read is still loading: don't flag a shortfall yet.\n  const insufficientBalance = parsedAmount !== undefined && usdcBalance !== undefined && usdcBalance < parsedAmount;","originalFile":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useMemo, useState } from \"react\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, isAddress, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PAYOUTS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts;\n\nconst shorten = (addr?: string) => (addr ? `${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writePayouts, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n  });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS.address],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: BigInt(PAYOUTS.deployedOnBlock),\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  // Parse the amount to USDC's 6 decimals. Returns undefined for empty/invalid input.\n  const parsedAmount = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!amount) return undefined;\n    try {\n      return parseUnits(amount, 6);\n    } catch {\n      return undefined;\n    }\n  }, [amount]);\n\n  const isValidRecipient = isAddress(recipient);\n  const needsApproval = parsedAmount !== undefined && (allowance ?? 0n) < parsedAmount;\n  const hasEnoughBalance = parsedAmount !== undefined && (usdcBalance ?? 0n) >= parsedAmount;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (parsedAmount === undefined || parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount to approve.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    try {\n      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [PAYOUTS.address, parsedAmount] });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (!isValidRecipient) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid recipient address.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (parsedAmount === undefined || parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    try {\n      await writePayouts({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC</p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-1\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            {recipient && !isValidRecipient && <p className=\"text-error text-sm mb-2\">Invalid address.</p>}\n\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mt-2 mb-1\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            {parsedAmount !== undefined && !hasEnoughBalance && (\n              <p className=\"text-error text-sm mb-2\">Insufficient USDC balance.</p>\n            )}\n\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mt-2 mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isApproving ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isApproving || parsedAmount === undefined || !needsApproval}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                {needsApproval ? \"Approve\" : \"Approved\"}\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPaying ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={\n                  isPaying || !isValidRecipient || parsedAmount === undefined || needsApproval || !hasEnoughBalance\n                }\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? `${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writePayouts, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n  });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS.address],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: BigInt(PAYOUTS.deployedOnBlock),\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  // Parse the amount to USDC's 6 decimals. []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={`${payout.transactionHash}-${payout.logIndex}` ?? i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">","originalFile":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useMemo, useState } from \"react\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, isAddress, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useScaffoldReadContract, useScaffoldWriteContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PAYOUTS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts;\n\nconst shorten = (addr?: string) => (addr ? `${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writePayouts, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n  });\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS.address],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: BigInt(PAYOUTS.deployedOnBlock),\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  // Parse the amount to USDC's 6 decimals. Returns undefined for empty/invalid input.\n  const parsedAmount = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!amount) return undefined;\n    try {\n      return parseUnits(amount, 6);\n    } catch {\n      return undefined;\n    }\n  }, [amount]);\n\n  const isValidRecipient = isAddress(recipient);\n  const needsApproval = parsedAmount !== undefined && (allowance ?? 0n) < parsedAmount;\n  // Undefined balance means the read is still loading: don't flag a shortfall yet.\n  const insufficientBalance = parsedAmount !== undefined && usdcBalance !== undefined && usdcBalance < parsedAmount;\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (parsedAmount === undefined || parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount to approve.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    try {\n      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [PAYOUTS.address, parsedAmount] });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (!isValidRecipient) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid recipient address.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (parsedAmount === undefined || parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid amount.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    try {\n      await writePayouts({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC</p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-1\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            {recipient && !isValidRecipient && <p className=\"text-error text-sm mb-2\">Invalid address.</p>}\n\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mt-2 mb-1\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            {insufficientBalance && <p className=\"text-error text-sm mb-2\">Insufficient USDC balance.</p>}\n\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mt-2 mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isApproving ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isApproving || parsedAmount === undefined || !needsApproval}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                {needsApproval ? \"Approve\" : \"Approved\"}\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPaying ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={\n                  isPaying || !isValidRecipient || parsedAmount === undefined || needsApproval || insufficientBalance\n                }\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n              {\" paid \"}\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>{\" \"}\n              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdM8bcwFQ3EEmdv4toDLi","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Committed cleanly — lint-staged re-ran eslint + typecheck as a pre-commit gate and both passed.\n\n## Summary\n\nThe `/payouts` page was feature-complete but had ship-blocking issues for a real-money app on Base mainnet. I fixed the code and config, verified with typecheck + lint, and committed to `main` (`54fdd10`).\n\n**What was broken & fixed** (`packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx`):\n- **Payout feed would hang/rate-limit** — `useScaffoldEventHistory` scanned `fromBlock: 0n`, i.e. ~33M Base blocks in 500-block batches on every load. Now scans from the contract's `deployedOnBlock` (33712450).\n- **Zero user feedback on payments** — used raw wagmi `useWriteContract` with only `console.error`. A failed or pending *payment* showed the user nothing. Switched to `useScaffoldWriteContract`, which gives tx toasts, error notifications, and target-network checks (also what AGENTS.md mandates).\n- **Approve/Pay flow was blind** — clicking \"Send Payout\" before approving just reverted. Added an allowance read so the Approve button reflects real state, plus `isAddress` recipient validation, a balance check, and safe amount parsing (bad decimal input no longer throws deep in wagmi).\n- Stable React `key` for the feed list; no false \"insufficient balance\" flash while the balance read is loading.\n\n**Config** (`packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`): lowered `pollingInterval` 30000 → 4000 so the L2 feed stays near-real-time (per AGENTS.md L2 guidance).\n\nI left `Payouts.sol` untouched — it's already deployed and correct for USDC (returns-bool `transferFrom`, no state/reentrancy surface).\n\n**Before you flip it live** (deployment config, not code — can't be committed here):\n- Set your own `NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY` and `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID` in Vercel. The repo falls back to shared/throttled demo keys, which will rate-limit under real traffic — especially now that polling is faster.\n- Longer term, the event-history feed is SE-2's documented \"not for production\" path. It's fine for launch volume, but if the payouts feed gets heavy, move it to an indexer (ponder/subgraph — both are available skills in this repo)."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2178,"cache_read_input_tokens":65271,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2178},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b119473c-3802-46cd-bab8-aad1df4e3744","uuid":"2adba79d-4195-4963-a458-aa0fdb12de4c","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:38:13.634Z","request_id":"req_011CdM8bXAUtfgfLWjzGJEUh"}
